## Narration 92

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A2176)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 13</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا الْقَعْنَبِيُّ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ تَسْأَلِ الْمَرْأَةُ طَلاَقَ أُخْتِهَا لِتَسْتَفْرِغَ صَحْفَتَهَا وَلْتَنْكِحْ فَإِنَّمَا لَهَا مَا قُدِّرَ لَهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2176]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2176" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2176</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2176 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah reported the Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ) as saying “A woman should not ask for the divorce of her sister to make her bowl vacant for her and to marry him. She will have what is decreed for her.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2176]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2176" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2176</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2176 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: औरत अपनी बहिन की तलाक़ का मुतालबा न करे ताकि उसका प्याला खाली करा ले (यानि उसका हिस्सा खुद ले ले) और खुद निकाह कर ले मुक़द्दर में होगा वो उसे मिलेगा.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2176]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2176" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2176 ]</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ عنہ کہتے ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: عورت اپنی بہن کی طلاق کا مطالبہ نہ کرے تاکہ اس کا پیالہ خالی کرا لے ( یعنی اس کا حصہ خود لے لے ) اور خود نکاح کر لے، جو اس کے مقدر میں ہو گا وہ اسے ملے گا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2176]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2176" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 93

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A2177)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 13</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ يُونُسَ، حَدَّثَنَا مُعَرِّفٌ، عَنْ مُحَارِبٍ، قَالَ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ مَا أَحَلَّ اللَّهُ شَيْئًا أَبْغَضَ إِلَيْهِ مِنَ الطَّلاَقِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2177]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2177" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2177</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2177 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Muharib: The Prophet (ﷺ) said: Allah did not make anything lawful more abominable to Him than divorce</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2177]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2177" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">मुहारीब कहते हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: अल्लाह त&#x27;आला के नज़दीक हलाल चीज़ों में तलाक़ से ज़्यादा न-पसंदीदा कोई चीज़ नहीं.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2177]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2177" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 94

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A2178)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 13</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا كَثِيرُ بْنُ عُبَيْدٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ، عَنْ مُعَرِّفِ بْنِ وَاصِلٍ، عَنْ مُحَارِبِ بْنِ دِثَارٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ أَبْغَضُ الْحَلاَلِ إِلَى اللَّهِ تَعَالَى الطَّلاَقُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2178]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2178" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

## Narration 95

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A2179)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 13</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا الْقَعْنَبِيُّ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ طَلَّقَ امْرَأَتَهُ وَهِيَ حَائِضٌ عَلَى عَهْدِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَسَأَلَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ مُرْهُ فَلْيُرَاجِعْهَا ثُمَّ لْيُمْسِكْهَا حَتَّى تَطْهُرَ ثُمَّ تَحِيضَ ثُمَّ تَطْهُرَ ثُمَّ إِنْ شَاءَ أَمْسَكَ بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ وَإِنْ شَاءَ طَلَّقَ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَمَسَّ فَتِلْكَ الْعِدَّةُ الَّتِي أَمَرَ اللَّهُ سُبْحَانَهُ أَنْ تُطَلَّقَ لَهَا النِّسَاءُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2179]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2179" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2179</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2179 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">‘Abd Allah bin Umar said that he divorced his wife while she was menstruating during the time of the Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ). So ‘Umar bin Al Khattab asked the Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ) about this matter. The Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ) said “Order him, he must take her back and keep her back till she is purified, then has another menstrual period and is purified. Thereafter if he desires he may divorce her before having intercourse with her, for that is the period of waiting which Allaah the Glorified has commanded for the divorce of women.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2179]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2179" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2179</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2179 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हुमा) से रिवायत है के उन्होंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के ज़माने में अपनी बीवी को हालत-ए-हैज़ में तलाक़ दे दी, तो उमर बिन ख़त्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से उसके मुतलिक दरयाफ्त किया, रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: उसे हुक्म दो के वो अपनी बीवी से रुजू कर ले, फिर उसे रोके रखे यहाँ तक के वो हैज़ से पाक हो जाये, फिर हैज़ आ जाये, फिर पाक हो जाये, फिर उसके बाद अगर चाहे तो रखे, वरना छूने से पहले तलाक़ दे दे, यही वो इद्दत है जिसका अल्लाह ताअला ने औरतों को तलाक़ देने के सिलसिले में हुक्म दिया है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2179]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2179" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے زمانے میں اپنی بیوی کو حالت حیض میں طلاق دے دی، تو عمر بن خطاب رضی اللہ عنہ نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے اس کے متعلق دریافت کیا، رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: اسے حکم دو کہ وہ اپنی بیوی سے رجوع کر لے، پھر اسے روکے رکھے یہاں تک کہ وہ حیض سے پاک ہو جائے، پھر حیض آ جائے، پھر پاک ہو جائے، پھر اس کے بعد اگر چاہے تو رکھے، ورنہ چھونے سے پہلے طلاق دیدے، یہی وہ عدت ہے جس کا اللہ تعالیٰ نے عورتوں کو طلاق دینے کے سلسلے میں حکم دیا ہے ۱؎ ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2179]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2179" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhuma) se riwayat hai ke unhone Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke zamane mein apni biwi ko halat-e-haiz mein talaq de di, toh Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se uske mutaliq daryaft kiya, Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Use hukm do ke wo apni biwi se rujoo kar le, phir use roke rakhe yahan tak ke wo haiz se paak ho jaye, phir haiz aa jaye, phir paak ho jaye, phir uske baad agar chahe toh rakhe, warna chune se pehle talaq de de, yahi wo iddat hai jiska Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne aurton ko talaq dene ke silsile mein hukm diya hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2179]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2179" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr">The aforesaid tradition has also been transmitted by Nafi’ through a different chain of narrators. This version says Ibn ‘Umar divorced a wife of his while she was menstruating pronouncing one divorce. He then narrated the rest of the tradition similar to the one narrated by Malik</p>

## Narration 97

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A2181)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 13</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عُثْمَانُ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا وَكِيعٌ، عَنْ سُفْيَانَ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، مَوْلَى آلِ طَلْحَةَ عَنْ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ طَلَّقَ امْرَأَتَهُ وَهِيَ حَائِضٌ فَذَكَرَ ذَلِكَ عُمَرُ لِلنَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ مُرْهُ فَلْيُرَاجِعْهَا ثُمَّ لْيُطَلِّقْهَا إِذَا طَهُرَتْ أَوْ وَهِيَ حَامِلٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2181]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2181" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2181</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2181 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn ‘Umar said that he divorced his wife while she was menstruating. ‘Umar mentioned the matter to the Prophet(ﷺ). He (the Prophet) said “Order him, he must take her back and divorce her when she is purified (from menstrual discharge) or she is pregnant.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2181]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2181" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2181</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2181 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि उन्होंने अपनी बीवी को हालत-ए-हैज़ में तलाक़ दे दी, तो उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से इसका ज़िक्र किया। रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: &#x27;उससे हुक्म दो कि वो रुजू कर ले, फिर जब वो पाक हो जाये या हामला (प्रेग्नेंट) हो, तो उसे तलाक़ दे।&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2181]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2181" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2181</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2181 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے اپنی بیوی کو حالت حیض میں طلاق دے دی تو عمر رضی اللہ عنہ نے نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے اس کا ذکر کیا، رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: اسے حکم دو کہ وہ رجوع کر لے پھر جب وہ پاک ہو جائے یا حاملہ ہو تو اسے طلاق دے ۱؎ ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2181]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2181" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ki unhone apni biwi ko halat-e-haiz mein talaq de di, toh Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se iska zikr kiya. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: &#x27;Usse hukum do ki woh ruju kar le, phir jab woh pak ho jaye ya hamla (pregnant) ho, toh use talaq de.&#x27;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2181]</p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 13</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ صَالِحٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَنْبَسَةُ، حَدَّثَنَا يُونُسُ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَخْبَرَنِي سَالِمُ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ طَلَّقَ امْرَأَتَهُ وَهِيَ حَائِضٌ فَذَكَرَ ذَلِكَ عُمَرُ لِرَسُولِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَتَغَيَّظَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ثُمَّ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مُرْهُ فَلْيُرَاجِعْهَا ثُمَّ لْيُمْسِكْهَا حَتَّى تَطْهُرَ ثُمَّ تَحِيضَ فَتَطْهُرَ ثُمَّ إِنْ شَاءَ طَلَّقَهَا طَاهِرًا قَبْلَ أَنْ يَمَسَّ فَذَلِكَ الطَّلاَقُ لِلْعِدَّةِ كَمَا أَمَرَ اللَّهُ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">‘Abd Allah (bin Umar) said that he divorced his wife while she was menstruating. ‘Umar mentioned the matter to the Messenger of Allah(ﷺ). The Messenger of Allah(ﷺ) became angry and said “Command him, he must take her back and keep her back till she is purified, then has another menstrual period and is purified. Then if he desires he may divorce her during the period of purity before he has intercourse with her. This is the divorce for waiting period as commanded by Allaah, the Exalted</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2182" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Ibn Umar’s event conveyed through Umar to the Prophet, followed by the printed instruction.. Read the complete Hindi field. Preserve the source divorce event, Umar’s report, anger, return instruction, successive periods of purity/menstruation and the final without-touching condition. Keep every supplied timing restriction without importing a different version.</p>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि उन्होंने अपनी बीवी को हालत-ए-हैज़ में तलाक़ दे दी। उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से इसका ज़िक्र किया तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) गुस्सा हो गये, फिर फरमाया: &#x27;उससे हुक्म दो कि इस से रुजू कर ले, फिर इसे रोके रखे यहाँ तक कि वो हैज़ से पाक हो जाये, फिर हैज़ा हो, फिर पाक हो जाये। फिर चाहिये कि वो पाकी की हालत में उसे छुए बग़ैर तलाक़ दे। यही इद्दत की तलाक़ है जैसा कि अल्लाह ने हुक्म दिया है।&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2182]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2182" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے اپنی بیوی کو حالت حیض میں طلاق دے دی، عمر رضی اللہ عنہ نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے اس کا ذکر کیا، تو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم غصہ ہو گئے پھر فرمایا: اسے حکم دو کہ اس سے رجوع کر لے پھر اسے روکے رکھے یہاں تک کہ وہ حیض سے پاک ہو جائے، پھر حائضہ ہو پھر پاک ہو جائے پھر چاہیئے کہ وہ پاکی کی حالت میں اسے چھوئے بغیر طلاق دے، یہی عدت کا طلاق ہے جیسا کہ اللہ نے حکم دیا ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2182]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2182" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ki unhone apni biwi ko halat-e-haiz mein talaq de di. Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se iska zikr kiya toh Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ghussa ho gaye, phir farmaya: &#x27;Usse hukum do ki is se ruju kar le, phir ise roke rakhe yahan tak ki woh haiz se pak ho jaye, phir haiza ho, phir pak ho jaye. Phir chahiye ki woh paki ki halat mein use chhue baghair talaq de. Yahi iddat ki talaq hai jaisa ki Allah ne hukum diya hai.&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2182" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا الْحَسَنُ بْنُ عَلِيٍّ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الرَّزَّاقِ، أَخْبَرَنَا مَعْمَرٌ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ، عَنِ ابْنِ سِيرِينَ، أَخْبَرَنِي يُونُسُ بْنُ جُبَيْرٍ، أَنَّهُ سَأَلَ ابْنَ عُمَرَ فَقَالَ كَمْ طَلَّقْتَ امْرَأَتَكَ فَقَالَ وَاحِدَةً ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Ibn ‘Umar (companion). This numbered source report retains the named Companion&#x27;s words or reported conduct under that attribution; it is not relabelled as the Prophet&#x27;s speech.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yunus bin Jubair said that he asked Ibn ‘Umar “How many times did you pronounce divorce to your wife? He replied, once.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2183]</p>

## Narration 100

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 13</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2184]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2184" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2184</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2184 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا الْقَعْنَبِيُّ، حَدَّثَنَا يَزِيدُ، - يَعْنِي ابْنَ إِبْرَاهِيمَ - عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ سِيرِينَ، حَدَّثَنِي يُونُسُ بْنُ جُبَيْرٍ، قَالَ سَأَلْتُ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ قَالَ قُلْتُ رَجُلٌ طَلَّقَ امْرَأَتَهُ وَهِيَ حَائِضٌ ‏.‏ قَالَ تَعْرِفُ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ قُلْتُ نَعَمْ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَإِنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ طَلَّقَ امْرَأَتَهُ وَهِيَ حَائِضٌ ‏.‏ فَأَتَى عُمَرُ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَسَأَلَهُ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مُرْهُ فَلْيُرَاجِعْهَا ثُمَّ لْيُطَلِّقْهَا فِي قُبُلِ عِدَّتِهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ قُلْتُ فَيُعْتَدُّ بِهَا قَالَ فَمَهْ أَرَأَيْتَ إِنْ عَجَزَ وَاسْتَحْمَقَ</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2184" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Retain the described divorce incident and Prophet&#x27;s explicit order. Later independent discussion of whether divorce counts remains notes.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yunus bin Jubair said “I asked ‘Abd Allah bin ‘Umar “A man divorced his wife while she was menstruating? He said do you know ‘Abd Allah bin ‘Umar? He said, yes. ‘Abd Allah bin ‘Umar divorced his wife while she was menstruating. So, ‘Umar came to the Prophet (ﷺ) and asked him (about this matter). He said Command him to take her back in marriage he may the divorce her in the beginning of the waiting period.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2184]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2184" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2184</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2184 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> I (Ibn Jubair) asked him “Will this divorce be counted? He said “Why not?” If he was helpless and showed his foolishness (that would have been counted)</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2184" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2184]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2184" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2184</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2184 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">मुहम्मद बिन सिरिन कहते हैं कि मुझे यूनुस बिन जुबैर ने बताया की मैंने अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से मसला दरयाफ्त किया. मैंने कहा कि जिस आदमी ने अपनी बीवी को हालात-इ-हैज़ में तलाक़ दे दी है (तोह उसका क्या हुकुम है?) कहने लगे की तुम अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) को पहचानते हो? मैंने कहा: हाँ. तोह उन्होंने कहा: अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने अपनी बीवी को हालात-इ-हैज़ में तलाक़ दे दी थी (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की खिदमत में हाज़िर हो कर मसला दरयाफ्त किया. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: &#x27;उससे हुकुम दो की वह रुजू कर ले और इद्दत के आग़ाज़ में उसे तलाक़ दे.&#x27; मैंने पूछा: क्या इस तलाक़ का शुमार होगा? उन्होंने कहा: तुम क्या समझते हो</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2184" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2184]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2184" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2184 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">محمد بن سیرین کہتے ہیں کہ مجھے یونس بن جبیر نے بتایا کہ میں نے عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما سے مسئلہ دریافت کیا، میں نے کہا کہ جس آدمی نے اپنی بیوی کو حالت حیض میں طلاق دے دی ہے ( تو اس کا کیا حکم ہے؟ ) کہنے لگے کہ تم عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما کو پہچانتے ہو؟ میں نے کہا: ہاں، تو انہوں نے کہا: عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما نے اپنی بیوی کو حالت حیض میں طلاق دے دی تو عمر رضی اللہ عنہ نے نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی خدمت میں حاضر ہو کر مسئلہ دریافت کیا، آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: اسے حکم دو کہ وہ رجوع کر لے اور عدت کے آغاز میں اسے طلاق دے ۔ میں نے پوچھا: کیا اس طلاق کا شمار ہو گا؟ انہوں نے کہا: تم کیا سمجھتے ہو اگر وہ عاجز ہو جائے یا دیوانہ ہو جائے ۱؎۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2184" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2184]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2184" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2184</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2184 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Muhammad bin Sirin kehte hain ki mujhe Yunus bin Jubair ne bataya ki maine Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se masla daryaft kiya. Maine kaha ki jis aadmi ne apni biwi ko halat-e-haiz mein talaq de di hai (toh uska kya hukum hai?) Kehne lage ki tum Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko pehchante ho? Maine kaha: Haan. Toh unhone kaha: Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne apni biwi ko halat-e-haiz mein talaq de di thi, toh Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki khidmat mein hazir ho kar masla daryaft kiya. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: &#x27;Usse hukum do ki woh ruju kar le aur iddat ke aaghaz mein use talaq de.&#x27; Maine pucha: Kya is talaq ka shumar hoga? Unhone kaha: Tum kya samajhte ho, agar woh aajiz ho jaye ya deewana ho jaye?</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2184" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>
